### K01 | VO1 "Bu Efe; altıncı sınıfa gidiyor ve okula her sabah yürüyerek gider." · yazı: "EFE" · KARAKTER
FİKİR: Efe yürürken çantasının kapağını çevirip defterine bakıyor — henüz bir şey yazmıyor, sadece dünkü satıra göz atıyor. Bu çocuk gördüğünü yazan çocuk; dersin motoru ilk karede tanıtılıyor.
PLAN:  kahraman @efe1 ve bib cebindeki defter · net insan: İKİ, ikisi de yüzüyle · ışık sabah güneşi sokağın ucundan geliyor, Efe'nin yanağını, defterin sayfasını ve arkadaki simitçinin tezgâhını alıyor, kaldırımın gölgeli yarısına hiç inmiyor · özne zeminden aydınlık yüz ve sayfa ile arkadaki koyu cephe arasındaki farkla ayrılıyor · FEDA: simitçinin yüzünün yarısı tezgâhın camının yansımasında kaybolur
⚠ @efe1 KADRAJ KİLİDİ: referans sayfasının "tam boy, ayakta, dinlenme pozunda" duruşu EZİLİR — burada yürüyor, gövdesi hafif öne eğik, ağırlığı ön ayakta, bir eli çantanın kapağında.
-----
[1 LENS]      50mm lens at f/2.8, chest height, medium three-quarter view walking with him — a parked car's wing mirror cuts the left foreground and is sliced by the frame edge
[2 SUBJECT]   — @efe1 walking to school with the flap of his satchel turned back in one hand, glancing down at the open notebook in his bib pocket rather than at the pavement ahead
[3 IDENTITY]  @efe1 in mid-stride, body angled slightly forward with his weight on the front foot and one hand holding the satchel flap open — deliberately not standing at rest, not centred and not in a full-figure studio pose. His mouth is closed, his eyes are down on the page, and one eyebrow is slightly raised as he re-reads something. Behind him at a corner stall, a Turkish/Anatolian street vendor of about sixty in a flat cap and a grey apron is stacking simit rings onto a glass-fronted cart, half his face lost in the reflection on the glass, the lit half squinting into the morning sun. Warm matte tan skin on both, low specular, never tinted green or grey; both faces modelled and readable.
[4 SETTING]   The setting is a city pavement early on a school morning, fully dressed: uneven paving slabs with one lifted by a root, a glass-fronted simit cart with its brass legs and a tray of sesame rings, a row of parked cars along the kerb with their wing mirrors folded in, a cast-iron street lamp, a green waste bin, a bicycle chained to a post, and every shopfront along the street closed behind a corrugated roller shutter with its awning furled and strapped to the frame, so no blank sign panel or empty fascia is left anywhere in the frame
[5 LIGHT]     A low morning sun comes down the length of the street from ahead of him; it takes his cheek and jaw, the open page in his bib pocket, the brass edge of the simit cart and the top curves of the sesame rings, and it reaches nothing on the shaded half of the pavement, which stays a deep cool indigo band
[7 THREE ALIVE] Three things are alive in the frame: the turned-back satchel flap still swinging from his stride, steam lifting off the fresh simit under the cart's glass, and a sparrow hopping along the kerb ahead of him.
[8 DEPTH]     Depth in three layers — the wing mirror blurred and cut at the left edge, @efe1 and the open notebook sharp in the middle, the simit vendor and the street beyond softening into gentle bokeh while staying fully modelled.
[9 CONTACT]   Every object rests in contact with its surface and casts a soft contact shadow — the simit cart's brass legs press into the paving with a small hard dark point beneath each, and the chained bicycle's tyre flattens slightly where it meets the post.
STYLE: Feature-animation 3D CGI in the RenderMan / RenderMan-successor premium-CG feature-animation pipeline lineage, subsurface scattering on character skin that carries the scene's light temperature, Every prop has a deliberate overscale factor (10-15%) for child-safe readability, Material specificity is non-negotiable, wood shows visible grain with satin-varnish sheen, fabric shows woven stitch texture and slight fiber fray at edges, complementary bounce fill at 25-35% intensity in the key's opposing hue, FULL 3D CGI FEATURE-ANIMATION RENDER, continuous physically-based shading. Silhouette reads entirely through lighting rim and value separation, Ambient occlusion painted in, never raytraced-hard.
LIGHT AND PALETTE: shadows read as deep cool indigo, midtones read as vivid warm burnt orange, accents read as vivid warm amber, highlights read as near-white warm ivory — palette character: warm-honey SSS dominant on skin; complementary cool-violet bounce in shadow; wood tones in brown-ochre range with satin sheen; fabric in warm-saturated primaries with fiber texture. NO acid green, NO neon, NO teal-orange Hollywood grade, NO desaturated grey anywhere in character or environment. Render these as light behaviour, never flat fills.
TEXT: The carrier is a cotton name tape stitched inside the turned-back flap of his satchel, the kind a parent sews in · the letterform is machine-embroidered block capitals in dark blue thread on white tape, each letter slightly raised and fuzzy at the edges where the thread ends, the tape itself frayed at one corner and washed soft · it reads "EFE" — one word, three letters, all capitals · positioned on the inside of the lifted flap, turned toward the lens by the way he holds it, small but legible · ORIENTATION: the word reads left-to-right in normal reading direction and is never mirrored, reversed, rotated or flipped; it appears on this one named object and on no other surface in the frame · any background sign soft-focus, Turkish or blank.
NEGATIVE: the notebook page in his pocket stays soft and no written line on it becomes legible; the simit cart carries no printed price list, brand or lettering; the parked cars show no number plates and no visible badges; both faces stay modelled and readable and neither flattens into a shape; neither of them looks at the lens; the roller shutters stay closed so no shop sign surface exists; NO photoreal or live-action capture and no real-human photographic skin — continuous 3D feature-animation CGI throughout; NO 2D cel shading, hard black outline or toon shader; NO hard-black shadow, no flat graphic fill; NO teal-orange grade, no desaturated look; Turkish text only, NO English signage; no franchise character, real person, logo or brand name.

### K02 | VO2 "O sabah köşedeki kaldırım rampasının önünde bir araba duruyordu." · yazı: YOK · KAVRAM
FİKİR: Araba rampanın tam üstünde ve ön tekerleği tırmanıp yarı yolda kalmış — sürücü acele park etmiş, kimse kasten kötülük yapmamış. Suç yok, düşüncesizlik var. Rampa arabanın altından görünüyor ve boşa çıkıyor.
PLAN:  kahraman rampayı kapatan ön tekerlek · net insan: BİR, yüzüyle (uzaklaşan sürücü) · ışık sabah güneşi kaldırım boyunca yalıyor, rampanın açıkta kalan yarısını ve tekerleğin üst kavisini alıyor, arabanın altına hiç girmiyor · özne zeminden aydınlık açık beton rampa ile arabanın altındaki karanlık boşluk arasındaki farkla ayrılıyor · FEDA: sürücünün yüzünün yarısı omzunun arkasında kalır
-----
[1 LENS]      35mm lens at f/4.0, low angle just above the kerb line, medium three-quarter view of the corner — the ramp's chipped near edge cuts the lower foreground and is sliced by the frame edge
[2 SUBJECT]   — a parked car standing across the dropped-kerb ramp with its front wheel half up the slope and stopped there, so the ramp runs out of use underneath it, and its driver already walking away up the pavement with a bag of bread
[3 IDENTITY]  One Turkish/Anatolian man of about forty-five, fully described and fully rendered: a zipped grey jacket over a shirt collar, keys still in his hand, a loaf in a paper bag under his arm, walking away with his head turned to check the shop door he is heading for — half his face is behind his own shoulder, and the visible half is unbothered rather than guilty. He is not a villain; he simply did not look down. Warm matte tan skin, low specular, never tinted green or grey; his face is modelled and readable.
[4 SETTING]   The setting is @kose in the morning: the dropped-kerb ramp with its two tactile grooves and chipped edges, the granite kerb, the plane tree in its iron guard, a green municipal waste bin, a cast-iron street lamp, a bus-stop pole whose timetable case is angled to the sky so nothing inside reads, a post box, a bicycle locked to the tree guard, and every shopfront closed behind a corrugated roller shutter with its awning furled and strapped to the frame, so no blank sign panel or empty fascia is left anywhere in the frame
[5 LIGHT]     A low morning sun rakes along the pavement from the left; it takes the exposed lower half of the ramp, the top curve of the front tyre, the kerb crown and the walking man's shoulder and cheek, and it reaches nothing under the car, which stays a deep cool indigo cavity
[7 THREE ALIVE] Three things are alive in the frame: a plane leaf turning over on the ramp's slope, the driver's keys still swinging from his finger, and warm exhaust shimmer thinning above the tailpipe.
[8 DEPTH]     Depth in three layers — the ramp's chipped near edge blurred and cut at the lower edge, the front wheel and the covered ramp sharp in the middle, the walking driver and the shopfronts softening into gentle bokeh while staying fully modelled.
[9 CONTACT]   Every object rests in contact with its surface and casts a soft contact shadow — the front tyre flattens visibly where it grips the ramp's slope and holds a dark crescent under its contact patch, and the locked bicycle leans into the tree guard with a hard dark point where they touch.

Silhouette reads entirely through lighting rim and value separation, Ambient occlusion painted in, never raytraced-hard.

NO acid green, NO neon, NO teal-orange Hollywood grade, NO desaturated grey anywhere in character or environment. Render these as light behaviour, never flat fills.

### K04 | VO4 "Arkasından gelen sınıf arkadaşı Derin ise dolanamadı." · yazı: YOK · KARAKTER
FİKİR: Derin'in tekerleği kaldırım taşının dibinde duruyor — bir parmak boyu bile çıkamaz. Ve el halkasındaki eli hâlâ itme pozisyonunda: pes etmemiş, sadece yol yok. Karenin gerilimi bir milimlik boşlukta.
PLAN:  kahraman tekerlek ile kaldırım taşı arasındaki bir parmaklık boşluk · net insan: BİR, yüzüyle (@derin) · ışık sabah güneşi el halkasının kromunu ve Derin'in yanağını alıyor, kaldırımın dibindeki gölgeye hiç inmiyor · özne zeminden parlayan krom halka ile koyu taş dibi arasındaki farkla ayrılıyor · FEDA: arabanın gövdesi kadrajın üst kenarında kesilir, hiç okunmaz

🔴 KAVRAMA ZORUNLU: elin halkayı nasıl tuttuğu yazılır — motorun en sık bozduğu yer.
-----

### K07 | VO7 "İnsanlar yaşamlarını toplum içinde, ortak alanları paylaşarak sürdürür." · yazı: "DURAK" · KAVRAM
FİKİR: Tek durakta beş ayrı hayat aynı üç metreyi paylaşıyor — biri okula, biri işe, biri pazara, biri bebek arabasıyla, biri köpeğiyle. Ortak alan bir kavram değil, dirseklerin birbirine değdiği bir genişliktir.
PLAN:  kahraman durağın dar sığınağı ve içindeki beş beden · net insan: BEŞ, hepsi yüzüyle · ışık sabah güneşi durağın çatısının altından girip beş yüzün alt yarısını ve bankın kenarını alıyor, arkadaki cepheye hiç çıkmıyor · özne zeminden aydınlık yüzler ve bank ile arkadaki koyu cephe arasındaki farkla ayrılıyor · FEDA: en sağdaki adamın yüzünün üst yarısı durak çatısının gölgesinde kalır
-----
[1 LENS]      35mm lens at f/2.8, seated eye level, medium-wide view into the shelter — the shelter's near upright post cuts the left foreground and is sliced by the frame edge
[2 SUBJECT]   — five people waiting inside one narrow bus shelter with their shoulders and bags overlapping, all facing the same direction up the road, none of them looking at each other
[3 IDENTITY]  Five Turkish/Anatolian people, all fully described and fully rendered. On the bench, a woman of about seventy in a headscarf and a long coat with a net bag of lemons on her knees, hands folded over the handles. Beside her a man of about thirty in a courier's fleece with a parcel under one arm, checking the road with his chin lifted. Standing, a woman of about thirty-five with one hand on a pram's handlebar and the other holding a travel mug, weight on one hip. Behind her a schoolgirl of about fourteen in a maroon jumper with her arms through both straps of her bag, mouth moving on something she is memorising. ### K48 | VO48 "Gönüllü olarak çevre temizliği çalışmasına katıldılar." · yazı: "GÖNÜLLÜ" · KAVRAM
FİKİR: İş bölüşülmüş ama kimse kimseye iyilik yapmıyor: çuvalın ağzı Derin'in dizlerinde iki eliyle gergin duruyor, Efe eğilip yassılttığını içine bastırıyor. Katılmak, sana uyan işi ÜSTLENMEKTİR — birinin senin yerine yapması değil. Çuvalın ağzı karenin en aydınlık, içi en karanlık yeri.
PLAN:  kahraman dizlerin üstünde gergin duran çuval ağzı · net insan: ÜÇ (@derin, @efe1, gönüllü genç), üçü de yüzüyle · ışık sabah güneşi kaldırıma yatık geliyor, tişörtün ön yüzünü, çuvalın gergin dokuma ağzını ve Derin'in ön kollarını alıyor, gönüllünün yüzüne ve çuvalın içine hiç girmiyor · özne zeminden aydınlık gergin dokuma ile içindeki kara boşluk arasındaki farkla ayrılıyor · FEDA: gönüllü gencin gözleri ışık bandının üstünde açık gölgede kalır — yalnız tişörtü ve ön kolları okunur; Efe'nin yüzünün yarısı kendi kolunun arkasında
⚠ @derin KADRAJ KİLİDİ: duruş EZİLİR — gövdesi öne eğik, dirsekleri dizlerinde, iki eli çuvalın ağzını iki yandan açık tutuyor; el halkaları serbest, sandalye düz slab üstünde durgun.
⚠ @efe1 KADRAJ KİLİDİ: duruş EZİLİR — iyice eğilmiş, bir eli dizine dayalı, öbür eliyle yassılttığı bir şişeyi çuvalın ağzından içeri bastırıyor.
🔴 KAVRAMA ZORUNLU: Derin'in her iki elinin başparmağı çuvalın kenarının İÇİNDE, dört parmağı dışında, dokuma başparmakların altında kırışıyor; bilekleri geriye yatık, ağırlık ön kollarında. Efe'nin eli yassı şişeyi orta parmak ile başparmak arasında tutuyor, işaret parmağı şişenin dip kenarına bastırıyor.
-----
[1 LENS]      50mm lens at f/2.8, low angle at seated chest height, close three-quarter view — a filled sack already knotted and standing on the paving cuts the left foreground and is sliced by the frame edge
[2 SUBJECT]   — a woven sack held open across @derin's knees with its mouth pulled taut between her two hands, @efe1 bent down pressing a flattened bottle in through the opening, and a young volunteer behind them twisting the neck of a sack she has already filled
[3 IDENTITY]  @derin bent forward out of her upright seated posture with her elbows on her knees and both hands holding the sack's mouth open — each thumb hooked inside the woven rim with four fingers outside it, the weave crumpling under both thumbs, her wrists laid back and the weight carried in her forearms — deliberately not sitting square and not in a full-figure studio pose; her hands are off the push-rims and the chair stands still on level slab. Her mouth is open on a short breath of effort and her eyes are on the opening, not on Efe. @efe1 is bent well over with one hand braced on his own knee, the other pressing the flattened bottle down through the sack's mouth, half his face behind his own upper arm. Behind them a Turkish/Anatolian young woman of about twenty in a plain screen-printed cotton tee stands with her chest square to the lens, both hands twisting the neck of a full sack closed, her forearms and the front of the tee lit while her eyes sit above the light band in open shade. Warm matte tan skin on all three, low specular, never tinted green or grey; all three faces modelled and readable.
[4 SETTING]   The setting is @kose's pavement early on a Saturday morning, fully dressed: the plane tree in its iron guard with swept leaves banked against the ironwork, the granite kerb, three more knotted sacks standing in a row along the kerb line, a stiff yard broom leaning against the tree guard, a pair of dropped work gloves on the slab, the cast-iron lamp, a bicycle locked to the guard, and every shopfront still closed behind a corrugated roller shutter with its awning furled and strapped to the frame, so no blank sign panel or empty fascia is left anywhere in the frame
[5 LIGHT]     A low morning sun comes along the pavement from the right; it takes the flat printed front of the volunteer's tee, the taut woven mouth of the sack across @derin's knees, her forearms and the back of Efe's pressing hand, and it reaches nothing on the volunteer's face, which stays in open shade above the light band, and nothing down inside the sack, which holds a deep cool indigo hole
[7 THREE ALIVE] Three things are alive in the frame: the sack's mouth flexing where her thumbs hold it, a plane leaf skidding along the kerb past the standing sacks, and the twisted neck of the volunteer's sack still unwinding a turn in her hands.
[8 DEPTH]     Depth in three layers — the knotted sack blurred and cut at the left edge, the open sack mouth and both pairs of working hands sharp in the middle, the volunteer and the shuttered fronts softening into gentle bokeh while staying fully modelled.
[9 CONTACT]   Every object rests in contact with its surface and casts a soft contact shadow — the standing sacks slump onto the paving with dark seams where they touch, the broom's bristles splay against the tree guard, and both of @derin's rear tyres flatten on the slab with dark contact patches.
STYLE: Feature-animation 3D CGI in the RenderMan / RenderMan-successor premium-CG feature-animation pipeline lineage, subsurface scattering on character skin that carries the scene's light temperature, Every prop has a deliberate overscale factor (10-15%) for child-safe readability, Material specificity is non-negotiable, wood shows visible grain with satin-varnish sheen, fabric shows woven stitch texture and slight fiber fray at edges, complementary bounce fill at 25-35% intensity in the key's opposing hue, FULL 3D CGI FEATURE-ANIMATION RENDER, continuous physically-based shading. Silhouette reads entirely through lighting rim and value separation, Ambient occlusion painted in, never raytraced-hard.
LIGHT AND PALETTE: shadows read as deep cool indigo, midtones read as vivid warm burnt orange, accents read as vivid warm amber, highlights read as near-white warm ivory — palette character: warm-honey SSS dominant on skin; complementary cool-violet bounce in shadow; wood tones in brown-ochre range with satin sheen; fabric in warm-saturated primaries with fiber texture. NO acid green, NO neon, NO teal-orange Hollywood grade, NO desaturated grey anywhere in character or environment. Render these as light behaviour, never flat fills.
TEXT: The carrier is the front of the young volunteer's plain cotton tee, its printed panel turned flat and square to the lens and pulled tight across her chest by the reach of her arms · the letterform is screen-printed block capitals in matte black ink sitting on top of the cotton weave, the ink cracked along one old fold line with the fibres showing through it, flexing with the fabric but held flat by the pull of her shoulders · it reads "GÖNÜLLÜ" — one word, seven letters, all capitals, the second a capital Ö with its two dots and the fourth and seventh capital Ü each with two dots · positioned across the chest of the tee, unobstructed by either arm and fully legible · ORIENTATION: the word reads left-to-right in normal reading direction and is never mirrored, reversed, rotated or flipped; it appears on this one garment and on no other surface in the frame · the sacks, the gloves and the broom carry no printed word or label · any background sign soft-focus, Turkish or blank.
NEGATIVE: the sacks carry no printed word, logo, label or municipal marking of any kind; the flattened bottle in his hand shows no label, embossed brand or barcode; @derin's hands stay on the sack's mouth and the sack is never taken from her, held for her or lifted off her knees by anyone else; her wardrobe stays the same rust-orange zip-up fleece over a plain cream long-sleeved tee and she wears no printed tee, vest or tabard; the separated-waste bins are not in view in this frame; all three faces stay modelled and readable and none flattens into a shape; nobody looks at the lens; the roller shutters stay closed so no shop sign surface exists; NO photoreal or live-action capture and no real-human photographic skin — continuous 3D feature-animation CGI throughout; NO 2D cel shading, hard black outline or toon shader; NO hard-black shadow, no flat graphic fill; NO teal-orange grade, no desaturated look; Turkish text only, NO English signage; no commercial or corporate brand logo, no franchise character, no real person.

### K49 | VO49 "Uygulanan çözüm, sonuçlarına bakılarak değerlendirilir." · yazı: YOK · KAVRAM
FİKİR: Değerlendirme bir grafik değil, tek bir kalem darbesi: Efe defterindeki dört maddeden BİRİNİN üstünü çiziyor, üçüne dokunmuyor. Çizilen satır ile çizilmeyen üçü aynı sayfada yan yana duruyor — sonuç budur. Ve karar veren yüz okunmuyor: kapüşonun gölgesi Efe'nin gözlerini alıyor, ışık sayfada.

⚠ @efe1 KADRAJ KİLİDİ: duruş EZİLİR — kapüşonu başında, gövdesi @derin'in sandalyesinin sırt kolluğuna yaslanmış, defteri sol avucunun üstünde açık tutuyor, sağ eli çiziyor.
⚠ @derin KADRAJ KİLİDİ: duruş EZİLİR — başı yana ve yukarı dönmüş, çenesi kalkık, aynı sayfaya bakıyor; bir eli el halkasında.

### K50 | VO50 "Bir ay sonra toplanan atık miktarı belirgin biçimde azalmıştı." · yazı: "KAĞIT" "CAM" "PLASTİK" (@kutu) · KAVRAM
FİKİR: Azalma bir grafik değil, kaldırımdaki bir hayalet: dört poşetin bir ay önce her sabah durduğu yerde beton hâlâ soluk bir dikdörtgen olarak lekeli — ama üstünde artık HİÇBİR ŞEY yok. Yeşil çöp kutusunun kapağı ilk kez tam kapanıyor; @kutu'nun kapak kavisleri bir aylık elden parlamış. Yokluğun bile bir izi var.
PLAN:  kahraman boşalmış leke dikdörtgeni ile ilk kez tam kapanan kapak · net insan: İKİ (@derin, yaşlı adam), ikisi de yüzüyle · ışık sabah güneşi lekeli betonun soluk yüzeyini, kapanmış kapağın üst kenarını, kapaklardaki parlak sürtme kavislerini, adamın ellerini ve şişenin camını, Derin'in yanağını alıyor, çınarın altına hiç girmiyor · özne zeminden aydınlık soluk beton dikdörtgeni ile çevresindeki koyu, yıllardır yürünmüş kaldırım arasındaki farkla ayrılıyor · FEDA: yaşlı adamın yüzü çınarın gölgesinde biçim olarak okunur ama ışıklı detayı yoktur — yalnız elleri ve şişe ışıkta; @derin'in ön tekerleği alt kenarda kesilir
⚠ @derin KADRAJ KİLİDİ: duruş EZİLİR — sandalye kutulara yanlamış, gövdesi geriye yaslı, çenesi kalkık, kapanmış kapağa bakıyor; bir avucu el halkasının üstünde açık.
🔴 KAVRAMA ZORUNLU: yaşlı adamın eli cam şişeyi boynundan tutuyor — başparmak ve işaret parmağı boyun halkasının altında kenetli, orta parmağı gövdeye yaslı, şişenin dibi kutunun ağzına bir parmak kala; ağırlık bileğinde, kol yarı gergin.
⚠ ARDIŞIK DURUM: bu kare bir ÖNCEKİ hâlin kanıtıdır ve geri sarılamaz — kaldırımda hiçbir poşet, hiçbir dökülme, taşan hiçbir kapak yoktur.

### K51 | VO51 "Sorun sürüyorsa plan yeniden gözden geçirilir ve geliştirilir." · yazı: "YAYA YOLU" · KAVRAM
FİKİR: Çözüm uygulandı ve yetmedi. Rampanın başına yeni bir levha dikilmiş — galvaniz direk hâlâ parlak, dibindeki beton hâlâ soluk ve yağmur görmemiş — ama rampanın üstünde yine bir araba var, aynasının ucu levhaya bir karış. Üstteki lamba ise artık gündüz SÖNÜK: biri düzeldi, biri düzelmedi. Efe defterinde bomboş bir sayfa açmış, kalemi kâğıda değmiş ve henüz hiçbir şey yazmamış.

⚠ @efe1 KADRAJ KİLİDİ: duruş EZİLİR — yana dönük, ağırlığı tek kalçada, defter sol avucunun üstünde açık, sağ elindeki kalem boş sayfaya değmiş ama DURMUŞ; başı levhaya değil arabaya çevrili.
⚠ @derin KADRAJ KİLİDİ: duruş EZİLİR — sandalye rampanın alt ucunda durmuş, gövdesi öne eğik, iki avucu el halkalarında, tekerler dönmemiş.

### K56 | VO56 "Peki sen çevrende hangi toplumsal sorunu fark ettin?" · yazı: YOK · KAVRAM
FİKİR: Kare bir şey göstermiyor. Efe kadrajın sol kenarında çömelmiş ve karşı köşede duran bir şeye bakıyor — o şey kadrajın DIŞINDA. Sağ üçte iki, güneşin yaladığı bomboş kaldırım. Sorunun cevabı karede yok, çünkü cevabı seyirci koyacak; bakılan yeri boş bırakmak, kameraya bakan bir çocuktan daha çok soru sorar.
PLAN:  kahraman kadrajın sağ yarısındaki boşluk · net insan: BİR, yüzüyle (@efe1, profilden ve yakın) · ışık sabah güneşi Efe'nin profilini — kaş, burun, üst dudak, çene önü — kapalı defterin kapağını ve boş kaldırımın taraklı yüzeyini boydan boya alıyor, çınarın uzun gölgesine ve karşı kaldırıma hiç ulaşmıyor · özne zeminden aydınlık profil ile arkasındaki gövde gölgesinin koyu oyuğu arasındaki farkla ayrılıyor · FEDA: kadrajın sağ yarısında hiçbir olay yok ve baktığı şey hiç görünmüyor; Efe'nin sol eli ve dizi kadrajın sol kenarında kesilir
⚠ @efe1 KADRAJ KİLİDİ: referans duruşu EZİLİR — tam boy ayakta değil, tek dizi kaldırıma değecek kadar çömelmiş, dirseği dizinde, defteri KAPALI ve koltuk altında sıkışmış, başı sağa, sokağın karşısına çevrilmiş.
🔴 KAVRAMA ZORUNLU: kapalı defterin nasıl sıkıştırıldığı ve yere basan elin nasıl yük aldığı yazılır.
-----
[1 LENS]      35mm lens at f/2.8, low at his crouched eye level, his near shoulder barely two paces from the lens so his profile reads large and sharp in the left third while the pavement runs away empty across the whole right two thirds — the plane tree's flaking trunk cuts the extreme left foreground and is sliced by the frame edge
[2 SUBJECT]   — @efe1 crouched at the foot of the plane tree with his notebook shut under one arm, stopped mid-walk and looking straight out across an empty stretch of pavement at something on the far corner that the frame never shows
[3 IDENTITY]  @efe1 down in a low crouch, weight forward, one elbow resting on his raised knee — deliberately not standing at rest and not in a full-figure studio pose. His grip is exact: the closed soft-cover notebook is clamped between his upper arm and his ribs with its furred corner poking out behind him and the pencil stub still on its cover clip, while his right hand is planted flat on the paving with the fingers spread and the fingertips blanched white where they carry part of his weight. His mouth is closed, his eyes are narrowed against the raking sun with one brow lower than the other, and his head is turned fully to the right, out of the frame, at something across the road. Warm matte tan skin, low specular, never tinted green or grey; his face is modelled and readable in full profile.
[4 SETTING]   The setting is @kose in the morning, fully dressed, and the right two thirds of the frame is one unbroken run of sunlit pavement with nothing standing on it: the combed brush texture of the paving slabs, one long plane-tree shadow lying across it, one pale square of fresh new concrete where the root-lifted slab has been replaced, a drain grate with a leaf caught in it, the worn granite kerb, and the dropped-kerb ramp completely clear at its far end with its two tactile grooves showing; behind the pavement the four-storey ochre and faded rose render, the cast-iron street lamp, the green waste bin with its lid down, and every shopfront closed behind a corrugated roller shutter with its awning furled and strapped to the frame, so no blank sign panel or empty fascia is left anywhere in the frame
[5 LIGHT]     A low morning sun rakes straight up the length of the pavement from the far corner toward the lens; it takes his brow, the ridge of his nose, his upper lip and the front of his jaw, the board cover of the notebook under his arm, the combed surface of every paving slab and the crown of the kerb, and it reaches nothing inside the plane tree's long shadow and nothing on the far pavement across the road, which stay a deep cool indigo
[7 THREE ALIVE] Three things are alive in the frame: a dry plane leaf skating a hand's width across the empty paving, a thin thread of dust turning slowly in the raking light, and a sparrow landing on the bare kerb halfway down the empty stretch.
[8 DEPTH]     Depth in three layers — the plane trunk blurred and cut at the extreme left edge, his crouched profile and the clamped notebook sharp in the near left third, the long empty pavement and the clear ramp at its far end softening into gentle bokeh while staying fully modelled.
[9 CONTACT]   Every object rests in contact with its surface and casts a soft contact shadow — his spread right hand presses into the paving with the pads of the fingers flattening under the load and a close dark contact shadow beneath the heel of the hand, and the closed notebook dents the fleece of his sweatshirt where his arm clamps it.
STYLE: Feature-animation 3D CGI in the RenderMan / RenderMan-successor premium-CG feature-animation pipeline lineage, subsurface scattering on character skin that carries the scene's light temperature, Every prop has a deliberate overscale factor (10-15%) for child-safe readability, Material specificity is non-negotiable, wood shows visible grain with satin-varnish sheen, fabric shows woven stitch texture and slight fiber fray at edges, complementary bounce fill at 25-35% intensity in the key's opposing hue, FULL 3D CGI FEATURE-ANIMATION RENDER, continuous physically-based shading. Silhouette reads entirely through lighting rim and value separation, Ambient occlusion painted in, never raytraced-hard.
LIGHT AND PALETTE: shadows read as deep cool indigo, midtones read as vivid warm burnt orange, accents read as vivid warm amber, highlights read as near-white warm ivory — palette character: warm-honey SSS dominant on skin; complementary cool-violet bounce in shadow; wood tones in brown-ochre range with satin sheen; fabric in warm-saturated primaries with fiber texture. NO acid green, NO neon, NO teal-orange Hollywood grade, NO desaturated grey anywhere in character or environment. Render these as light behaviour, never flat fills.
TEXT: No text anywhere in this frame — the question is the part of the frame that is missing, and any legible word would answer it before the viewer can. The notebook stays shut so no page shows, the replaced concrete square stays plain, the roller shutters stay down and the timetable case returns only sky. Any background sign soft-focus, Turkish or blank.

### K57 | VO57 "Cevaplarınızı yorumlara bekliyoruz; meraklı kalın, çözüm üretmeye devam edin!" · yazı: "RAMPA" · "ÇÖP" · "LAMBA" · KARAKTER
FİKİR: Defter ilk kez BİZE dönüyor. Üç satır kurşun kalemle dolu ve her birinin yanında çetele var; dördüncü satır boş ve kalemin ucu tam onun başında duruyor. Karenin en aydınlık yeri o boş satır — video biterken sırada duran şey, seyircinin yazacağı satırdır.
PLAN:  kahraman bize çevrilmiş defter sayfası ve üstündeki boş dördüncü satır · net insan: İKİ, ikisi de yüzüyle (@efe1 net, @derin yumuşak ama tam modelli) · ışık sabah güneşi kâğıdın dokusunu ve grafit satırların metalik parlağını yandan alıyor, boş dördüncü satırı karenin en parlak yeri bırakıyor, Efe'nin belinin altına ve arkadaki kaldırıma hiç inmiyor · özne zeminden aydınlık beyaz kâğıt ile arkadaki koyu oker cephe arasındaki farkla ayrılıyor · FEDA: Efe'nin gövdesi göbeğinden aşağı kadraj tarafından kesilir ve Derin'in yüzünün yarısı kaldırılmış sayfanın kenarının arkasında kalır
⚠ @efe1 / @derin KADRAJ KİLİDİ: ikisinin de referans duruşu EZİLİR — Efe rampanın tepesinde, ağırlığı tek kalçasında, gövdesi bize dörtte üç dönük, defteri döndürüp kaldırmış; Derin sandalyesini rampanın tepesinde çeyrek dönmüş, gövdesi eğime karşı hafif geriye yatık.
🔴 KAVRAMA ZORUNLU: defteri tutan sol el ve kalemi tutan sağ el ayrı ayrı yazılır; kalemin ucu kâğıda GERÇEKTEN değer.
-----
[1 LENS]      50mm lens at f/2.8, chest height, close-medium three-quarter view at the top of the clear ramp — the cast-iron street lamp's fluted base cuts the left foreground and is sliced by the frame edge
[2 SUBJECT]   — @efe1 holding his open notebook turned flat toward the lens with the pencil tip already resting at the start of an empty fourth ruled line, and his eyes lifted off the page straight into the lens
[3 IDENTITY]  @efe1 standing at the crest of the clear ramp with his weight on one hip and his torso turned three-quarters to us, the notebook brought up and around so the page faces the lens — deliberately not the reference sheet's full-figure resting pose, and his body is cut by the frame below the waist. His grip is exact: the notebook lies open across his left palm with the spine in the crease of his fingers, his left thumb hooked over the outer edge of the near page to hold it flat and well clear of the writing, the other four fingers spread beneath the back cover; his right hand holds the pencil stub in an ordinary writing tripod, thumb and index on the shaft and the chewed end against the pad of his middle finger, and the graphite tip is already touching the paper at the left end of the empty line so the sheet dimples very slightly under it. His mouth is closed, one eyebrow is raised and one corner of his mouth is pulled in — an invitation, not a smile for a camera. Behind him at the crest of the ramp @derin has turned her chair a quarter toward him, her near hand still closed on the push-rim with the palm open and the thumb along its top edge holding the chair against the slope, her face lit down one side with the other half behind the raised page, also looking into the lens. Warm matte tan skin on both, low specular, never tinted green or grey; both faces modelled and readable.
[4 SETTING]   The setting is @kose in the morning with the ramp clear: the dropped-kerb ramp's pale concrete with its two tactile grooves running down behind them, the worn granite kerb, the plane tree in its low iron guard, the green municipal waste bin with its lid down, the cast-iron street lamp on its fluted base, the bus-stop pole whose timetable case is angled to the sky so nothing inside reads, a post box, a bicycle locked to the tree guard, and every shopfront closed behind a corrugated roller shutter with its awning furled and strapped to the frame, so no blank sign panel or empty fascia is left anywhere in the frame

[7 THREE ALIVE] Three things are alive in the frame: the free half of the page trembling against his thumb, a small curl of graphite dust shifting on the paper beside the pencil tip, and the plane tree's shadow easing a hand's width across the pavement behind them.
[8 DEPTH]     Depth in three layers — the lamp's fluted base blurred and cut at the left edge, the open page and both his hands sharp in the middle, @derin and the clear ramp behind her softening into gentle bokeh while staying fully modelled.
[9 CONTACT]   Every object rests in contact with its surface and casts a soft contact shadow — the pencil's graphite tip genuinely touches the paper and leaves one tiny dark point of contact with a hair of dust around it, the notebook's board cover presses a shallow dent into his palm, and @derin's rear tyre flattens on the ramp's concrete where it holds her weight against the slope.

Beneath the third word a fourth ruled line is left completely empty, with nothing on it but the pencil's graphite tip resting at its left end · positioned in the middle of the frame with the page turned flat and square to the lens, large enough that all three words, all three tally columns and the empty line read clearly, and nothing crosses the page · ORIENTATION: every word reads left-to-right in normal reading direction, sits level along its own ruled line, and is never mirrored, reversed, rotated or flipped; the words appear on this one notebook page and on no other surface in the frame · any background sign soft-focus, Turkish or blank.
